<file path="prisma/schema.prisma">
// prisma/schema.prisma

generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  // Lembre-se: Mantenha ou remova essa linha dependendo se está usando Prisma 6 ou a configuração do Prisma 7
  //url      = env("DATABASE_URL")
}

// ==========================================
// ENUMS
// ==========================================
enum Role {
  SUPER_ADMIN   // Dono do SaaS (Ação Leve / Você)
  ORG_ADMIN     // O Cliente (Contratante da ONG/Igreja)
  STAFF         // Identidade global para a equipe (Vendedores, Fiscais, Locutores)
}

enum PrizeType {
  QUINA
  FULL_HOUSE
}

enum PaymentMethod {
  PIX
  CASH   // Dinheiro vivo
  CARD   // Débito/Crédito
}

enum PlanType {
  SINGLE_EVENT   // Plano por Evento Único
  ANNUAL         // Plano Anual Livre
}

// ==========================================
// MODELS
// ==========================================

// 🏢 TENANT (Inquilino do SaaS)
model Tenant {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  name      String
  subdomain String   @unique
  logoUrl   String?
  active    Boolean  @default(true)
  token     String?
  
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
  planType  PlanType @default(SINGLE_EVENT)
  expiresAt DateTime? // Data limite real do acesso

  users     User[]
  events    Event[]

  @@map("tenants")
}

// 👤 USERS (Identidade Global no Inquilino)
model User {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  name      String?
  email     String?  @unique  
  username  String?  @unique  // Credencial de acesso (Ex: DALFERNA)
  password  String?
  role      Role     @default(ORG_ADMIN)
  
  tenantId  String?
  tenant    Tenant?  @relation(fields: [tenantId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  // Histórico de alocações desta pessoa nos eventos
  staffAssignments EventStaff[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([tenantId])
  @@map("users")
}

// 🎉 EVENTS (O Evento de Bingo)
model Event {
  id                String   @id @default(cuid())
  tenantId          String
  name              String
  status            String   @default("DRAFT")
  drawnNumbers      Int[]    @default([])
  isActive          Boolean  @default(false)
  showBoard         Boolean  @default(true)
  ticketPrice       Float    @default(10.00)
  pixKey            String?
  
  // Celebração em 3 vias
  pendingWinnerCard String?
  pendingWinnerName String?
  bingoConfirmed    Boolean  @default(false)
  bingoConfirmedAt  DateTime?

  tenant            Tenant   @relation(fields: [tenantId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  
  // Relações
  staff             EventStaff[]
  cards             Card[]
  sponsors          Sponsor[]
  prizes            Prize[]
  transactions      Transaction[] 

  createdAt         DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt         DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([tenantId])
  @@map("events")
}

// 🚀 EVENT STAFF (A Escala e Permissões no Evento - Substitui o Seller)
model EventStaff {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  eventId    String
  userId     String
  
  // 🔥 PERMISSÕES DINÂMICAS PARA *ESTE* EVENTO
  canSell    Boolean  @default(true)
  canOperate Boolean  @default(false)
  canVerify  Boolean  @default(false)

  event      Event    @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  user       User     @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  
  // Rastreio financeiro e de estoque atribuído a esta pessoa neste evento
  cards        Card[]
  transactions Transaction[]

  createdAt  DateTime @default(now())

  // Trava de integridade: O mesmo usuário não pode ser adicionado duas vezes no mesmo evento
  @@unique([eventId, userId])
  @@map("event_staff")
}

// 🤝 SPONSORS (Patrocinadores do Telão)
model Sponsor {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  eventId      String
  name         String
  logoUrl      String?
  contribution Int      @default(0)
  order        Int      @default(0)
  prizes       Prize[]  
  event        Event    @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([eventId])
  @@map("sponsors")
}

// 🎟️ CARDS (Cartelas)
model Card {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  shortId      String   @unique
  matrix       Json
  isSold       Boolean  @default(false)
  
  // Aponta para a escala (EventStaff) em vez do antigo Seller
  eventStaffId String?
  isPaid       Boolean  @default(false)
  price        Float?

  isDemoCard   Boolean  @default(false)

  eventId      String
  event        Event       @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  staff        EventStaff? @relation(fields: [eventStaffId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  
  transactions Transaction[] 

  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([eventId])
  @@index([shortId])
  @@map("cards")
}

// 🏆 PRIZES (Rodadas/Prêmios)
model Prize {
  id          String    @id @default(cuid())
  eventId     String
  name        String
  type        PrizeType
  prizeName   String
  order       Int
  isCompleted Boolean   @default(false)
  winnerId    String?
  
  sponsorId   String?
  sponsor     Sponsor?  @relation(fields: [sponsorId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  
  event       Event     @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@map("prizes")
}

// 💰 TRANSACTIONS (Extrato Financeiro)
model Transaction {
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  eventId      String
  cardId       String?
  
  // Aponta para a escala (EventStaff) em vez do antigo Seller
  eventStaffId String?
  
  amount       Float
  method       PaymentMethod
  
  event        Event         @relation(fields: [eventId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  card         Card?         @relation(fields: [cardId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)
  staff        EventStaff?   @relation(fields: [eventStaffId], references: [id], onDelete: SetNull)

  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())

  @@map("transactions")
}

// 🛡️ AUDIT LOGS (Registro de Integridade)
model AuditLog {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  action    String
  userId    String?
  tenantId  String?
  metadata  Json?
  
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([tenantId])
  @@map("audit_logs")
}
</file>

<file path="src/proxy.ts">
// src/proxy.ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { auth } from "@/lib/auth";

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    "/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.svg).*)",
  ],
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// HELPER: Resolve subdomínio
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function resolveHost(hostname: string): string {
  const host = hostname.split(":")[0];

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "production") {
    const rootDomain = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ROOT_DOMAIN || "acaoleve.com";
    if (host === rootDomain || host === `www.${rootDomain}`) return "root";
    const baseDomain = rootDomain.split(".").slice(1).join(".");
    return host.replace(`.${baseDomain}`, "");
  }

  if (host === "localhost") return "root";
  if (host.endsWith(".localhost")) return host.replace(".localhost", "");
  return host;
}

// Rotas públicas (marketing + algumas públicas por tenant)
const rotasPublicasMarketing = ["/", "/termos", "/privacidade"];
const rotasPublicasPorTenant = ["/entrar", "/projector", "/verify", "/cartela"];

export default auth((req) => {
  const { pathname } = req.nextUrl;
  const currentHost = resolveHost(req.headers.get("host") || "");
  const session = req.auth;
  const role = session?.user?.role ?? "";
  const userSubdomain = session?.user?.subdomain;

  // 1. ROTAS INSTITUCIONAIS (Marketing)
  if (rotasPublicasMarketing.includes(pathname)) {
    if (currentHost !== "root") {
      const rootUrl = new URL(pathname, req.url);
      rootUrl.host = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production"
        ? (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_ROOT_DOMAIN || "acaoleve.com")
        : "localhost:3000";
      return NextResponse.redirect(rootUrl);
    }
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  // 2. DOMÍNIO PRINCIPAL (Admin)
  if (currentHost === "root") {
    if (pathname.startsWith("/admin")) {
      if (!session) return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/admin/login", req.url));
      if (role !== "SUPER_ADMIN") return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/", req.url));
      return NextResponse.next();
    }
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  // 3. SUBDOMÍNIOS DE CLIENTES
  // Rotas Públicas por Tenant
  if (rotasPublicasPorTenant.some(route => pathname === route || pathname.startsWith(route))) {
    if (pathname === "/entrar" && session) {
      // Redirecionamento inteligente após login
      if (role === "OPERATOR") return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/live", req.url));
      if (role === "VERIFIER") return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/vendas", req.url)); // Verifier vai direto pro PDV
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/dashboard", req.url));
    }
    return NextResponse.next(); // Permite acesso público (projector, verify, etc)
  }

  // 3.2 ROTAS PROTEGIDAS
  if (!session) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/entrar", req.url));
  }

  // Trava de Tenant (Multi-tenancy)
  if (userSubdomain !== currentHost && role !== "SUPER_ADMIN") {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/entrar", req.url));
  }

  // === REGRAS POR ROLE ===

  // OPERATOR (Locutor) → Só pode acessar /live
  if (role === "OPERATOR") {
    if (!pathname.startsWith("/live")) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/live", req.url));
    }
  }

  // VERIFIER → Pode acessar /vendas e /verify
  if (role === "VERIFIER") {
    if (!pathname.startsWith("/vendas") && !pathname.startsWith("/verify")) {
      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL("/vendas", req.url));
    }
  }

  // ORG_ADMIN → Acesso total (menos /live se quiser)
  if (role === "ORG_ADMIN" || role === "SUPER_ADMIN") {
    // Pode tudo
  }

  // Reescrita interna para App Router
  const rewriteUrl = req.nextUrl.clone();

// Evita duplicar o subdomínio quando já está na URL
if (!pathname.startsWith(`/${currentHost}`)) {
  rewriteUrl.pathname = `/${currentHost}${pathname}`;
} else {
  rewriteUrl.pathname = pathname;
}

return NextResponse.rewrite(rewriteUrl);
});
</file>
